### [1] [Agentic Planning for Symbolic Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06397)
*Daniel Koh Ji Yang,Yannic Noller,Corina S. Pasareanu,Youcheng Sun*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: Agolic 是一种面向有界符号执行的智能规划系统，通过分析先前运行的证据来决定后续运行的目标与配置，从而在不改变底层状态探索机制的情况下提升分支覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次符号执行运行受资源限制，难以覆盖大量可达程序行为；现有工具可能仍有未被充分利用的潜力。研究旨在通过跨运行规划与配置，互补地扩展符号执行的实际覆盖范围。

Method: Agolic 使用智能代理整合源代码、重放后的覆盖信息以及历史目标尝试结果，并据此规划后续有界符号执行运行。其规划策略、可用证据和执行模式可针对不同符号执行工具及分析目标进行适配。论文重点评估了基于大语言模型的分支覆盖适配方案，并在多个 C/C++ 程序上进行实验，同时与持续符号执行、覆盖引导模糊测试和基于编译器的具体-符号执行结果进行比较。

Result: 在所有测试程序上，Agolic 都超过了连续符号执行获得的分支覆盖率，平均覆盖分支数超过其三倍。它覆盖的分支数也高于评估中的各个模糊测试和编译器具体-符号执行语料库，并在七个程序中的六个上到达了所有对比语料库合并后仍未覆盖的分支。

Conclusion: 跨运行地推理工具的使用方式能够显著挖掘现有符号执行工具的潜在能力。将高层规划交给智能代理、而保留常规状态选择与探索过程给底层工具，是提升符号执行覆盖效果的一种有效途径。

Abstract: Symbolic execution seeks to explore feasible program paths, yet a practical run may exhaust its resources while much program behaviour remains unreached. We investigate a complementary way of extending its practical reach by reasoning about how the same tool is utilised from one bounded run to the next, while leaving ordinary state exploration to the underlying tool. We present Agolic, an agentic planning system that uses evidence from earlier runs to choose and configure later bounded symbolic execution (BSE) runs, which the underlying symbolic execution tool then carries out. The planning intelligence, available evidence and execution modes can be adapted to the symbolic execution tool and analysis objective. We evaluate one adaptation for branch-coverage exploration, in which an LLM-based agent reasons over source code, replayed coverage and earlier targeting attempts. We evaluate Agolic on several C and C++ programs. On every program, it extends the branch coverage obtained by continuous symbolic execution and covers more than $3\times$ as many branches on average. It also covers more branches than each individual corpus from coverage-guided fuzzing and compiler-based concolic execution in our evaluation and reaches branches absent from all comparison corpora combined on six of the seven programs. Taken together, these results point to considerable untapped potential in existing symbolic execution tools, some of which may be realised by reasoning about how their capabilities are used across runs while leaving state selection during ordinary symbolic exploration to the underlying tool.

</details>


### [2] [PyFlow: An Inter-procedural Static Analysis Framework for Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07026)
*Zinan Gu,Haoxiang Yan,Peisen Yao*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出 PyFlow，一个面向 Python 的通用 IFDS 静态分析框架，并以污点分析验证其有效性。实验显示，PyFlow 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上均取得较高的召回率与 F1 分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python 具有动态分派、一等函数、元编程、广泛异常处理以及基于描述符和属性查找的对象模型，使精确的过程间静态分析十分困难。现有分析工具通常需要较多专用实现，且在覆盖率与精度之间存在权衡。

Method: PyFlow 采用多阶段中间表示流水线和可参数化抽象域的通用 IFDS 求解器。分析开发者仅需定义数据流语义，框架负责构建超图、执行不动点迭代并缓存过程摘要。论文实现了污点分析，并与 DevSkim、Dlint、Bandit、Bearer、CodeQL、Pysa、Semgrep 和 Snyk 八种 Python SAST 工具进行比较。

Result: 在合成基准上，PyFlow 在九种工具中获得最高的总体召回率和 F1 分数。在真实世界基准上，PyFlow 同样取得最高召回率和 F1 分数，同时保持了与基于污点分析引擎相当的精确率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，IFDS 框架能够有效支持 Python 的过程间污点分析，并通过统一的基础设施降低新分析的开发成本。PyFlow 在检测覆盖率方面表现突出，但其通用性、动态语言建模能力及在更大规模真实项目上的性能仍值得进一步评估。

Abstract: Static program analysis infers program properties automatically. Yet precise interprocedural analysis remains challenging, and dynamically typed languages amplify the difficulty. Python is particularly problematic: dynamic dispatch, first-class functions, metaprogramming, pervasive exceptions, and an object model based on descriptors and attribute-driven lookup collectively impede precise reasoning.
  We present PyFlow, a generic IFDS-based static-analysis framework for Python. PyFlow provides a multi-stage intermediate-representation pipeline and a generic IFDS solver parameterized by abstract domains. Analysis developers implement only the dataflow semantics; the framework constructs the supergraph, performs fixed-point iteration, and caches summaries.
  We implement a taint analysis in \pyflow and evaluate it against eight Python SAST tools (DevSkim, Dlint, Bandit, Bearer, CodeQL, Pysa, Semgrep, and Snyk) on the synthetic and real-world benchmarks from a recent ICSE~'26 study. On the synthetic benchmark, PyFlow achieves the best aggregate recall and F1 score among all nine tools. On the real-world benchmark, it attains the highest recall and F1 score while maintaining precision competitive with taint-based engines. We conclude with lessons learned from building IFDS analyses for Python.

</details>

### [3] [Endpoint Sufficiency Behavioral Quotients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06386)
*David Carr*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究何时可以安全遗忘生成系统中的来源信息而不改变其合法未来行为，并建立了从启用规则、有限轨迹到分支结构的严格判定层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一可见对象可能对应多个来源不同的出现，而这些出现的后续可用规则可能不同。因此，需要精确判断哪些来源区别仍具有行为意义，避免无条件保留或无条件丢弃来源信息。

Method: 将合法生成建模为带标签迁移系统，并使用端点投影将出现映射为可见对象。分别定义启用充分性、轨迹充分性和商充分性；在线性层面比较有限轨迹等价，在分支层面使用强互模拟。进一步构造端点等价与轨迹等价的交集，以及最大的端点保持互模拟关系；对有限系统采用以端点类为初始划分的分区细化算法。

Result: 三种观察层次严格递进。端点轨迹充分性当且仅当端点等价包含于有限轨迹等价；规范端点商具有代表元无关性，当且仅当端点等价是强互模拟等价。轨迹层面的交集是保持有限轨迹的最大端点保持关系，最大的端点保持互模拟给出最粗的精确分支商，并具有普适性。示例中，两个具有相同可见图I->A->B的出现因后续启用规则不同，在第一次细化中即被区分。

Conclusion: 来源信息能否被遗忘不应作全有或全无的处理，而应依据行为等价性判断。仅保留对未来启用情况、有限轨迹或分支结构仍有影响的来源区别，即可获得精确且尽可能粗的端点抽象。

Abstract: A provenance-decorated generative system may contain distinct occurrences with the same visible endpoint. The central abstraction question is therefore exact: when may provenance be forgotten without changing the lawful future? We study this question through the labeled transition system induced by lawful generation and an endpoint projection U from occurrences to visible objects.
  Three observation levels are separated. Enabled sufficiency preserves immediately available rule labels; trace sufficiency preserves all finite lawful rule-label traces; quotient sufficiency preserves the branching transition structure modulo endpoint equivalence. The resulting hierarchy is strict. At the linear-time level, endpoint trace sufficiency is exactly inclusion of endpoint equivalence in finite-trace equivalence. At the branching level, the canonical endpoint quotient is representative-independent exactly when endpoint equivalence is a strong bisimulation equivalence.
  Two canonical repairs follow. Intersecting endpoint equivalence with trace equivalence gives the greatest endpoint-respecting relation preserving finite traces. The greatest endpoint-respecting bisimulation gives the maximally coarse exact branching quotient and is universal among endpoint-respecting exact quotients. For finite systems it is computed by a terminating partition-refinement procedure initialized by endpoint classes. A self-contained application to provenance-decorated nested recursive-recombinant generation exhibits two occurrences with the same visible graph I->A->B but different enabled futures; the refinement procedure separates them in its first round. The result replaces an all-or-nothing demand to retain provenance with an exact criterion for retaining only the distinctions that remain behaviorally operative.

</details>


### [4] [Recovering Explanations from Transformed Rule-Based Ontologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06399)
*Alex Ivliev,Markus Krötzsch,Maximilian Marx*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将基于重写后Datalog规则得到的蕴含证明，转换为基于原始规则的对应证明，并分析该问题的计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规则重写可提高知识图谱本体推理的效率，但通常会破坏原始推导结构。虽然重写规则下的证明能够说明事实为何成立，却难以直接生成符合原始规则语义的解释，因此需要研究证明恢复与转换方法。

Method: 形式化从重写规则下的蕴含证明构造原始规则证明的问题，分析其计算复杂度，并提出两种适用于实际场景的证明转换语言，用于描述和指定转换过程。

Result: 研究建立了证明转换问题的复杂度结论，并识别出两种具有实践价值的语言，可用于规范从重写规则证明到原始规则证明的转换。摘要未给出具体复杂度类别或实验结果。

Conclusion: 规则优化不仅应保持蕴含事实，还应支持可解释的原始推导恢复。本文为重写规则环境下的证明转换提供了复杂度分析和形式化工具。

Abstract: Datalog rules are often used to define ontologies over Knowledge Graphs. Rule reasoners routinely optimise such ontologies by rewriting their rules into a form that can be evaluated more efficiently. These transformations preserve the entailed facts, but not the structure of the underlying derivations. A proof tree under the rewritten rules explains why a fact holds, but does not readily yield an explanation in terms of the original rules. We study the problem of constructing, from a proof of entailment under the rewritten rules, a proof under the original ones: we establish its computational complexity and identify two practically relevant languages for specifying proof transformations.

</details>


### [5] [Solution Space Partitioning for Extremal Set Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06432)
*Jesse Looney,Jonah McDonald,Allison Klingler,Gloria Wu,Jonad Pulaj,Haoze Wu*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向极值集合论的解空间划分方法，通过分析候选解的构造策略进行分类，并结合可生成精确证明的混合整数线性规划求解器，验证了比以往更大的Chvátal猜想有限情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用的解空间划分方法（如前瞻法）在极值集合论问题中的分解效率有限；同时，Chvátal猜想的有限规模验证仍受计算复杂度和证明可验证性的限制。

Method: 根据候选解的可能构造策略进行专门化的案例分析和解空间划分，并将该策略与能够输出精确证明的MILP求解器结合，用于自动化验证极值集合论中的有限实例。

Result: 实验表明，该方法相比领域无关的前瞻式划分能够更有效地分解极值集合论问题，并成功验证了规模大于既有工作的Chvátal猜想有限情形。

Conclusion: 针对问题结构设计解空间划分策略，并结合证明生成型MILP求解器，可以提升极值集合论计算验证的规模与效率，为研究相关猜想提供更强的计算辅助工具。

Abstract: We present a method for partitioning the solution space of statements in extremal set theory. Compared with domain-agnostic partitioning methods like look-ahead, we perform case analysis on the strategies by which a candidate solution can be constructed. We demonstrate that our approach can decompose problems in extremal set theory more effectively than look-ahead. Combining this new partitioning strategy with an exact proof-producing MILP solver, we are able to verify larger finite cases of Chvátal's Conjecture---a long-standing open question in extremal combinatorics---compared to previous work.

</details>


### [6] [Learning GR(1) Specifications from Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06546)
*Sam Nicholas Kouteili,William Fishell,Mark Santolucito,Ruzica Piskac*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出GR1MINE，一种基于SAT的GR(1)约束规格挖掘工具，可从系统轨迹示例中高效学习可实现的GR(1)时序逻辑公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用LTL规格挖掘的搜索空间大、计算开销高，而GR(1)作为支持多项式时间综合的LTL片段，适合表达大量软硬件系统中的假设—保证性质，因此需要针对该结构设计更高效的挖掘方法。

Method: GR1MINE利用GR(1)公式的时序骨架，采用增量式SAT求解枚举候选公式；通过复用SAT求解过程中学习到的子句，避免不同候选之间的重复计算，并以示例轨迹约束候选公式的正确性及可实现性。

Result: 在Boolean GR(1) Syntech基准上，GR1MINE能够为全部60个基准学习到可实现公式，速度相比通用和受约束LTL挖掘工具提升超过30倍。在SYNTCOMP的非GR(1)规格上，该工具在限定时间内恢复的可实现规格数量也比基线方法多超过2倍。

Conclusion: 通过将GR(1)的结构性限制与增量SAT求解结合，GR1MINE显著提升了规格挖掘的效率和成功率；其结果表明，面向特定可综合时序逻辑片段的专用挖掘方法优于通用LTL挖掘方案。

Abstract: Constrained specification mining enables the automatic discovery of desired properties from system traces. Generalized Reactivity of Rank 1, or GR(1), is a fragment of LTL with polynomial-time synthesis that natively encodes assume-guarantee properties present in most hardware and robotics domains. In this paper, we present GR1MINE, a SAT-based tool for efficiently learning GR(1) formulas from examples. We exploit the GR(1) temporal skeleton to incrementally enumerate formula candidates, leveraging learnt clauses to avoid recomputation. On the Boolean GR(1) Syntech suite, GR1MINE learns a realizable formula for all 60 benchmarks over 30X faster than generic and constrained LTL mining tools. On non-GR(1) specifications from SYNTCOMP, GR1MINE is still able to recover >2X more realizable specifications than baselines within the timeout.

</details>


### [7] [Embedding Modal Logics into Logics of Bunched Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07203)
*Daniele Sansoni,Ranald Clouston*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文给出将经典模态逻辑S4嵌入布尔分丛蕴涵逻辑（BBI）的全新纯句法证明，基于Hilbert演算，并借鉴哥德尔关于直觉主义命题逻辑嵌入S4的证明思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有嵌入证明主要依赖语义方法，难以直接处理带有假设及分丛结构的推理，也不一定适用于逻辑的公理扩展。本文旨在建立更一般、可组合且对公理扩展稳定的句法证明框架。

Method: 构造Hilbert风格演算，给出BBI中完整的演绎定理证明，并利用这些定理证明嵌入在普通假设和分丛组织的假设下均保持有效。研究还比较嵌入与两种逻辑语义性质之间的关系，并将方法推广到混合BBI、经典BI和次经典BBI等语言扩展。

Result: 获得了S4到BBI的纯句法嵌入证明；该证明对S4的任意公理扩展保持稳定，并适用于文献中已知的BBI公理扩展。相关演绎定理得到完整证明，且嵌入可自然推广至多种BBI扩展语言。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个比既有语义证明更普适的句法嵌入框架，统一处理假设、分丛结构和公理扩展问题，并说明了S4与BBI之间嵌入关系的语义基础及其推广能力。

Abstract: We present a new proof of the embedding of the classical modal logic S4 into the logic of Boolean Bunched Implications (BBI). While the original proof is semantical, this proof is entirely syntactical. It proceeds via Hilbert-style calculi, and is built by analogy with a recently discovered proof by Godel of the embedding of intuitionistic propositional logic into S4. We present the first full proofs of deduction theorems for BBI, which are used to show that the embedding is preserved by reasoning with assumptions, including where those assumptions are organised into bunches. Unlike the existing proof, our proof is stable under arbitrary axiomatic extensions of S4, and applies to all known axiomatic extensions of BBI in the literature. We observe how this embedding is related to semantical properties of both logics. Moreover, the proof extends gracefully to language extensions of BBI, as we show with hybrid BBI, classical BI, and sub-classical BBI.

</details>


### [8] [From the Dirichlet Integral to Lobachevsky's Formula: A Formalization in Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07366)
*Daniel Goldberg,Antoine Vinciguerra*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文在Lean 4中形式化狄利克雷积分及其若干经典应用，重点解决条件收敛积分、截断极限、参数微分与傅里叶逼近等证明难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sinc函数在正半轴上不具备Lebesgue可积性，因此狄利克雷积分不能直接作为无穷区间积分处理。论文旨在为这类条件收敛分析结果建立严格、可机器验证的形式化证明，并扩展到截断核收敛和三角积分恒等式等应用。

Method: 作者将狄利克雷积分表示为有限区间截断积分的极限。为避免直接处理条件收敛积分中指数因子的移除，先研究绝对可积的sinc²函数；通过积分号下求导和控制收敛定理计算其积分，再利用截断积分之间的恒等式恢复狄利克雷积分。随后，证明狄利克雷截断核收敛到Heaviside函数，导出二次与双线性三角积分公式；最后借助Mathlib在加法圆上的傅里叶分析所得余弦多项式稠密性，形式化满足反射对称性的连续周期函数的Lobachevsky积分公式。

Result: 论文完成了狄利克雷积分的Lean 4形式化，并建立了sinc²积分求值、狄利克雷截断到Heaviside函数的收敛、多个二次和双线性三角积分恒等式，以及具有反射对称性连续周期函数的Lobachevsky积分公式。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了Lean 4与Mathlib能够处理包含条件收敛、极限交换、参数化积分和傅里叶逼近的经典实分析问题。其核心策略是以绝对可积的sinc²作为中介，绕开直接操作条件收敛积分的技术障碍，并为后续形式化调和分析与积分变换相关结果提供基础。

Abstract: We formalize the Dirichlet integral and several of its classical applications in the Lean~4 proof assistant. Since the sinc function is not Lebesgue integrable on the positive half-line, the Dirichlet integral must be represented as the limit of integrals over bounded intervals. To avoid the difficulty of removing an exponential factor from a conditionally convergent integral, we instead pass through the absolutely integrable function \(\operatorname{sinc}^2\). We evaluate its integral by differentiation under the integral sign and dominated convergence, and then recover the Dirichlet integral from an identity between truncated integrals. Using these results, we formalize the convergence of the Dirichlet cutoff to the Heaviside function and derive several quadratic and bilinear trigonometric integral identities. Finally, we formalize Lobachevsky's integral formula for continuous periodic functions satisfying a reflection symmetry, using the density of cosine polynomials obtained from Mathlib's Fourier analysis on the additive circle.

</details>


### [9] [A Formalization of the Laplace Transform and Its Inversion in Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07384)
*Daniel Goldberg,Antoine Vinciguerra*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文在Lean 4中形式化了复值函数的拉普拉斯变换、基本运算规则及一种基于Bromwich公式的逆变换定理，并将其应用于简谐振子的拉普拉斯域求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为拉普拉斯变换及其逆变换建立可靠的形式化基础，弥合经典分析理论与定理证明器之间的差距，同时验证相关工程化应用的可形式化性。

Method: 在Lean 4中定义复值函数的拉普拉斯变换，证明线性、微分等基本运算规则；通过实变量积分理论和Dirichlet积分建立Bromwich型逆变换定理；随后形式化简谐振子的拉普拉斯域求解，并将所得变换与sin(ωt)的拉普拉斯变换对应起来。

Result: 完成了拉普拉斯变换及其主要运算规则的形式化，证明了Bromwich型逆变换结果，并成功形式化简谐振子的拉普拉斯域解，确认其对应于sin(ωt)的变换。研究还总结了复分析、积分理论、收敛性处理及Lean形式化过程中的主要挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了Lean 4形式化拉普拉斯变换理论及其应用的可行性，为进一步形式化积分变换、逆变换定理和微分方程求解提供了基础。

Abstract: We present a Lean 4 formalization of the Laplace transform for complex-valued functions, its fundamental operational rules, and a Bromwich-type inversion theorem proved through real-variable integration and the Dirichlet integral. As an application, we formalize the Laplace-domain solution of the harmonic oscillator and identify its transform with that of $\sin(ωt)$. We also discuss the principal analytic and formalization challenges encountered in the development.

</details>
